`apps/admin/src/api/lists.ts`(새, 전체):
```ts
import { useInfiniteQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, type Query } from './client';
import type { BeforeIdPage } from './types';

/**
 * 최근 것부터 "더 보기" 목록(서버: ?beforeId=&limit= → { items, nextBeforeId }, decisions/0039).
 * 쪽마다 따로 캐시하고 화면은 pages를 이어 붙여 보여 준다. 조건(query)이 바뀌면 처음부터.
 */
export function useBeforeIdList<T>(key: string, path: string, query: Query = {}) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: [key, query],
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => api<BeforeIdPage<T>>(path, { query: { ...query, beforeId: pageParam ?? undefined } }),
    initialPageParam: null as number | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextBeforeId,
  });
}

/** 이어 붙인 항목 */
export function itemsOf<T>(data: { pages: BeforeIdPage<T>[] } | undefined): T[] {
  return data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];
}
```

`apps/admin/src/components/MoreButton.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
/** "더 보기" 버튼. 다음 쪽이 없으면 "끝"을 보여 준다 */
export function MoreButton({
  hasNextPage,
  isFetchingNextPage,
  fetchNextPage,
}: {
  hasNextPage: boolean;
  isFetchingNextPage: boolean;
  fetchNextPage: () => unknown;
}) {
  if (!hasNextPage) return <p className="muted">끝</p>;
  return (
    <button type="button" disabled={isFetchingNextPage} onClick={() => void fetchNextPage()}>
      {isFetchingNextPage ? '불러오는 중' : '더 보기'}
    </button>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/ServiceTicketsPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { itemsOf, useBeforeIdList } from '../api/lists';
import { ErrorBox } from '../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../components/MoreButton';
import { WriteButton } from '../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst } from '../lib/format';
import { label } from '../lib/labels';

export interface TicketView {
  id: number;
  serialNo: string;
  merchantCode: string;
  symptom: string;
  status: string;
  remoteNote: string | null;
  replacementSerialNo: string | null;
  outboundShipmentId: number | null;
  outboundTrackingNo: string | null;
  openedBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
  closedAt: string | null;
}

export const TICKET_STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  OPEN: '접수됨',
  RESOLVED_REMOTELY: '원격 해결',
  REPLACING: '교체 출고됨',
  CLOSED: '종결',
};

/** A/S 접수 목록과 접수(F7). 접수는 운영 중(IN_SERVICE)인 단말기만 된다 — 서버가 422로 알려 준다 */
export function ServiceTicketsPage() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [serialNo, setSerialNo] = useState('');
  const [symptom, setSymptom] = useState('');
  const list = useBeforeIdList<TicketView>('service-tickets', '/service-tickets', { status });
  const open = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<TicketView>('/service-tickets', { method: 'POST', body: { serialNo: serialNo.trim(), symptom: symptom.trim() } }),
    onSuccess: async (ticket) => {
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['service-tickets'] });
      await navigate(`/service-tickets/${ticket.id}`);
    },
  });

  return (
    <section>
      <h2>A/S</h2>
      <div className="panel">
        <h3>접수</h3>
        <div className="row">
          <input aria-label="고장 단말기 시리얼" placeholder="시리얼" value={serialNo} onChange={(e) => setSerialNo(e.target.value)} />
          <input aria-label="증상" placeholder="증상" value={symptom} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => setSymptom(e.target.value)} />
          <WriteButton className="primary" disabled={!serialNo.trim() || !symptom.trim() || open.isPending} onClick={() => open.mutate()}>
            접수
          </WriteButton>
        </div>
        <ErrorBox error={open.error} />
      </div>
      <div className="filters">
        <select aria-label="접수 상태" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">상태 전체</option>
          {Object.entries(TICKET_STATUS_LABEL).map(([code, name]) => (
            <option key={code} value={code}>
              {name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>번호</th>
            <th>단말기</th>
            <th>가맹점</th>
            <th>증상</th>
            <th>상태</th>
            <th>접수</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {itemsOf(list.data).map((t) => (
            <tr key={t.id}>
              <td>
                <Link to={`/service-tickets/${t.id}`}>#{t.id}</Link>
              </td>
              <td>
                <Link to={`/terminals/${encodeURIComponent(t.serialNo)}`}>{t.serialNo}</Link>
              </td>
              <td>{t.merchantCode}</td>
              <td>{t.symptom}</td>
              <td>{label(TICKET_STATUS_LABEL, t.status)}</td>
              <td>
                {formatKst(t.createdAt)} · {t.openedBy}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/ServiceTicketDetailPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import type { TerminalDetail, TerminalPage } from '../api/types';
import { ErrorBox } from '../components/ErrorBox';
import { WriteButton } from '../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst } from '../lib/format';
import { CARRIER_LABEL, label } from '../lib/labels';
import { TICKET_STATUS_LABEL, type TicketView } from './ServiceTicketsPage';

/**
 * A/S 한 건(F7): 원격 점검(해결이면 종결) → 안 되면 교체 출고("새 단말기 출고 + 고장 단말기 회수 요청"을 서버가 한 트랜잭션으로) → 설치 확인.
 * 교체 단말기 후보는 고장 단말기와 같은 모델의 재고(IN_STOCK)다.
 */
export function ServiceTicketDetailPage() {
  const id = Number(useParams().id);
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [resolved, setResolved] = useState(false);
  const [replacement, setReplacement] = useState('');
  const [carrier, setCarrier] = useState('BUNGAE');

  const ticket = useQuery({ queryKey: ['service-ticket', id], queryFn: () => api<TicketView>(`/service-tickets/${id}`) });
  const t = ticket.data;
  const faulty = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['terminal', t?.serialNo, 'detail'],
    queryFn: () => api<TerminalDetail>(`/terminals/${encodeURIComponent(t!.serialNo)}`),
    enabled: t?.status === 'OPEN',
  });
  const stock = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['terminals', { status: 'IN_STOCK', modelCode: faulty.data?.modelCode }],
    queryFn: () => api<TerminalPage>('/terminals', { query: { status: 'IN_STOCK', modelCode: faulty.data!.modelCode, size: 50 } }),
    enabled: faulty.data !== undefined,
  });

  const act = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ path, body }: { path: string; body?: unknown }) => api<TicketView>(`/service-tickets/${id}/${path}`, { method: 'POST', body: body ?? {} }),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      setNote('');
      setReplacement('');
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['service-ticket', id] });
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['service-tickets'] });
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['terminals'] });
    },
  });

  return (
    <section>
      <p>
        <Link to="/service-tickets">← A/S 목록</Link>
      </p>
      <h2>A/S #{id}</h2>
      <ErrorBox error={ticket.error} />
      {t ? (
        <>
          <div className="panel">
            <dl className="info">
              <dt>상태</dt>
              <dd>
                <strong>{label(TICKET_STATUS_LABEL, t.status)}</strong>
              </dd>
              <dt>단말기</dt>
              <dd>
                <Link to={`/terminals/${encodeURIComponent(t.serialNo)}`}>{t.serialNo}</Link> ({t.merchantCode})
              </dd>
              <dt>증상</dt>
              <dd>{t.symptom}</dd>
              <dt>원격 점검</dt>
              <dd>{t.remoteNote ?? '-'}</dd>
              <dt>교체 단말기</dt>
              <dd>{t.replacementSerialNo ? `${t.replacementSerialNo} (운송장 ${t.outboundTrackingNo})` : '-'}</dd>
              <dt>접수</dt>
              <dd>
                {formatKst(t.createdAt)} · {t.openedBy}
              </dd>
              <dt>종결</dt>
              <dd>{formatKst(t.closedAt)}</dd>
            </dl>
          </div>
          <ErrorBox error={act.error} />
          {t.status === 'OPEN' ? (
            <>
              <div className="panel">
                <h3>원격 점검</h3>
                <textarea aria-label="점검 메모" rows={2} maxLength={500} value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} />
                <div className="row">
                  <label>
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={resolved} onChange={(e) => setResolved(e.target.checked)} /> 원격으로 해결됨(종결)
                  </label>
                  <WriteButton disabled={!note.trim() || act.isPending} onClick={() => act.mutate({ path: 'remote-checks', body: { resolved, note: note.trim() } })}>
                    점검 기록
                  </WriteButton>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="panel">
                <h3>교체 출고</h3>
                <div className="row">
                  <select aria-label="교체 단말기" value={replacement} onChange={(e) => setReplacement(e.target.value)}>
                    <option value="">같은 모델 재고에서 선택</option>
                    {stock.data?.items.map((s) => (
                      <option key={s.serialNo} value={s.serialNo}>
                        {s.serialNo}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <select aria-label="교체 택배사" value={carrier} onChange={(e) => setCarrier(e.target.value)}>
                    {Object.entries(CARRIER_LABEL).map(([code, name]) => (
                      <option key={code} value={code}>
                        {name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <WriteButton
                    className="primary"
                    disabled={!replacement || act.isPending}
                    onClick={() => act.mutate({ path: 'replacements', body: { replacementSerialNo: replacement, carrier } })}
                  >
                    교체 출고
                  </WriteButton>
                </div>
                {stock.data && stock.data.items.length === 0 ? <p className="muted">같은 모델의 재고가 없습니다.</p> : null}
              </div>
            </>
          ) : null}
          {t.status === 'REPLACING' ? (
            <div className="panel">
              <h3>설치 확인</h3>
              <p className="muted">교체 단말기가 가맹점에 설치됐으면 확인합니다(교체 단말기 → 운영 중, 접수 종결).</p>
              <WriteButton className="primary" disabled={act.isPending} onClick={() => act.mutate({ path: 'installations' })}>
                설치 확인
              </WriteButton>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/RolloutsPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { itemsOf, useBeforeIdList } from '../api/lists';
import { ErrorBox } from '../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../components/MoreButton';
import { WriteButton } from '../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst } from '../lib/format';
import { label } from '../lib/labels';

export interface StageStats {
  stage: number;
  percent: number;
  targets: number;
  offered: number;
  succeeded: number;
  failed: number;
  timedOut: number;
}

export interface RolloutView {
  id: number;
  modelCode: string;
  version: string;
  status: string;
  stages: number[];
  currentStage: number;
  failureThreshold: number;
  minSamples: number;
  stageTimeoutMs: number;
  stageStartedAt: string;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
  finishedAt: string | null;
  endReason: string | null;
  stageStats: StageStats[];
}

const ROLLOUT_STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  RUNNING: '진행 중',
  PAUSED: '멈춤',
  COMPLETED: '완료',
  ABORTED: '중단',
};

/** 펌웨어 단계 배포 현황(F8): 배포마다 단계별 대상·성공·실패·시간 초과, 멈춤·재개·중단 */
export function RolloutsPage() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const list = useBeforeIdList<RolloutView>('firmware-rollouts', '/firmware-rollouts', { status });
  const control = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, action }: { id: number; action: 'pause' | 'resume' | 'abort' }) =>
      api<RolloutView>(`/firmware-rollouts/${id}/${action}`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['firmware-rollouts'] }),
  });
  const abort = (r: RolloutView) => {
    if (window.confirm(`배포 #${r.id} ${r.version}을(를) 중단할까요? 되돌릴 수 없습니다.`)) control.mutate({ id: r.id, action: 'abort' });
  };

  return (
    <section>
      <h2>펌웨어 배포</h2>
      <div className="filters">
        <select aria-label="배포 상태" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">상태 전체</option>
          {Object.entries(ROLLOUT_STATUS_LABEL).map(([code, name]) => (
            <option key={code} value={code}>
              {name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void list.refetch()}>
          새로고침
        </button>
      </div>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error ?? control.error} />
      {itemsOf(list.data).map((r) => (
        <article key={r.id} className="panel" aria-label={`배포 ${r.id}`}>
          <h3>
            #{r.id} {r.modelCode} {r.version} <span className="badge">{label(ROLLOUT_STATUS_LABEL, r.status)}</span>
          </h3>
          <p className="muted">
            {r.stages.join('% → ')}% · 지금 {r.currentStage + 1}단계({formatKst(r.stageStartedAt)}부터) · 실패율 기준 {Math.round(r.failureThreshold * 100)}%, 최소 {r.minSamples}건 ·{' '}
            {r.createdBy} {formatKst(r.createdAt)}
            {r.finishedAt ? ` · 끝 ${formatKst(r.finishedAt)}` : ''}
          </p>
          {r.endReason ? <p>끝난 이유: {r.endReason}</p> : null}
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>단계</th>
                <th>누적 %</th>
                <th>대상</th>
                <th>보고 대기</th>
                <th>성공</th>
                <th>실패</th>
                <th>시간 초과</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {r.stageStats.map((s) => (
                <tr key={s.stage} aria-current={s.stage === r.currentStage ? 'step' : undefined}>
                  <td>{s.stage + 1}</td>
                  <td>{s.percent}</td>
                  <td>{s.targets}</td>
                  <td>{s.offered}</td>
                  <td className="ok">{s.succeeded}</td>
                  <td className={s.failed > 0 ? 'bad' : undefined}>{s.failed}</td>
                  <td>{s.timedOut}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          <div className="actions">
            {r.status === 'RUNNING' ? (
              <WriteButton disabled={control.isPending} onClick={() => control.mutate({ id: r.id, action: 'pause' })}>
                멈춤
              </WriteButton>
            ) : null}
            {r.status === 'PAUSED' ? (
              <WriteButton disabled={control.isPending} onClick={() => control.mutate({ id: r.id, action: 'resume' })}>
                재개
              </WriteButton>
            ) : null}
            {r.status === 'RUNNING' || r.status === 'PAUSED' ? (
              <WriteButton className="danger" disabled={control.isPending} onClick={() => abort(r)}>
                중단
              </WriteButton>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </article>
      ))}
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router';
import { Layout } from './components/Layout';
import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { RolloutsPage } from './pages/RolloutsPage';
import { ScanPage } from './pages/ScanPage';
import { ServiceTicketDetailPage } from './pages/ServiceTicketDetailPage';
import { ServiceTicketsPage } from './pages/ServiceTicketsPage';
import { TerminalDetailPage } from './pages/TerminalDetailPage';
import { TerminalListPage } from './pages/TerminalListPage';

/** 화면 경로. 메뉴(nav.ts)와 함께 고친다 */
export function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route index element={<HomePage />} />
        <Route path="terminals" element={<TerminalListPage />} />
        <Route path="terminals/:serialNo" element={<TerminalDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="scans" element={<ScanPage />} />
        <Route path="service-tickets" element={<ServiceTicketsPage />} />
        <Route path="service-tickets/:id" element={<ServiceTicketDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="firmware-rollouts" element={<RolloutsPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<p>없는 화면입니다.</p>} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  );
}
```

### Task 8: 리포트 화면 — 이상 단말기, VAN 대조(목록·상세), 발주 입고 대조, 주간 리포트

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/admin/src/components/RunningPoller.tsx`, `apps/admin/src/pages/reports/{AnomaliesPage,VanRunsPage,VanRunDetailPage,PurchaseOrdersPage,WeeklyReportsPage}.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/admin/src/App.tsx`, `apps/admin/src/nav.ts`
- Test: `apps/admin/src/pages/reports/ReportsPages.test.tsx`
- Record: `docs/devlog/2026-10-27.md`, `docs/decisions/0041-…`(보탬)

바꾸는 것 요약:
- 이상 단말기: 규칙 필터(이름·기준일은 `GET /anomaly-rules`), 열림 필터(기본 열린 것), afterId로 이어 보기(서버 오름차순 키셋), "지금 찾기"(동기 200 → 요약 `role=status`).
- VAN 대조: 실행 목록(더 보기), "대조 실행"(202, 수집 급감 확인 체크), 진행 중(RUNNING)인 실행이 있으면 실행 버튼을 끄고 2초마다 다시 읽는다(`RunningPoller`). 상세: 요약(단계 시간·실패 이유), 종류 필터(실행의 `counts` 키), 재검증 필터(기본 어긋남만), 결과 `kindLabel`, afterId 더 보기, RUNNING이면 다시 읽기.
- 발주 입고 대조: 발주하기(모델·수량), 발주 목록(더 보기), 고른 발주의 대조(발주·명세·입고 수, 차이, 빠진 시리얼, 명세 밖 거절), 마감(확인).
- 주간 리포트: 보낼 주(기본 지난주 월요일 KST — `lastWeekMonday`), 보내기(202 → "대기열에 넣었습니다"), 주 목록(`before` 더 보기), 대기열(`queued`)이 있으면 2초마다 다시 읽기, xlsx 내려받기 링크(`/api/reports/weekly/<주>/file`), 못 보낸 주는 "다시 보내기".

`apps/admin/src/pages/reports/ReportsPages.test.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { loginAs, mockApi, renderPage } from '../../test/render';
import { AnomaliesPage } from './AnomaliesPage';
import { PurchaseOrdersPage } from './PurchaseOrdersPage';
import { VanRunDetailPage } from './VanRunDetailPage';
import { VanRunsPage, type VanRunView } from './VanRunsPage';
import { lastWeekMonday, WeeklyReportsPage, type WeeklyReportView } from './WeeklyReportsPage';

describe('이상 단말기 (F4)', () => {
  it('규칙 이름·기준일은 서버 목록으로, 기본은 열린 것. 규칙을 고르면 다시 부르고, 지금 찾기는 요약을 보여 준다', async () => {
    const { calls } = mockApi({
      'GET /anomaly-rules': () => ({
        body: [
          { rule: 'DELIVERED_NOT_INSTALLED', label: '배송 완료 후 미설치', days: 7 },
          { rule: 'IN_SERVICE_NO_MERCHANT', label: '운영 중 가맹점 없음', days: null },
        ],
      }),
      'GET /anomalies': () => ({
        body: {
          items: [
            {
              id: 1,
              serialNo: 'AN-1',
              status: 'DELIVERED',
              rule: 'DELIVERED_NOT_INSTALLED',
              ruleLabel: '배송 완료 후 미설치',
              detectedAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z',
              lastSeenAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z',
              resolvedAt: null,
              detail: { days: 9 },
            },
          ],
          nextAfterId: null,
        },
      }),
      'POST /anomaly-scans': () => ({ body: { detected: 2, continuing: 5, resolved: 1, newSerials: ['AN-2', 'AN-3'] } }),
    });
    loginAs();
    renderPage(<AnomaliesPage />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('배송 완료 후 미설치 · 9일')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: '배송 완료 후 미설치 (7일)' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: '운영 중 가맹점 없음' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls.find((c) => c.path === '/anomalies')!.query.get('open')).toBe('true');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('규칙'), 'DELIVERED_NOT_INSTALLED');
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/anomalies').at(-1)!.query.get('rule')).toBe('DELIVERED_NOT_INSTALLED'));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '지금 찾기' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('새로 2건, 계속 5건, 풀림 1건');
  });
});

const run = (over: Partial<VanRunView> = {}): VanRunView => ({
  id: 3,
  status: 'COMPLETED',
  trigger: 'MANUAL',
  requestedBy: 'kim',
  acknowledgeDrop: false,
  startedAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z',
  heartbeatAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z',
  finishedAt: '2026-10-27T00:01:00.000Z',
  pages: 2,
  fetched: 800,
  retries: 1,
  candidates: 5,
  confirmed: 3,
  resolved: 2,
  unverifiable: 0,
  counts: { NOT_REGISTERED: 1, MERCHANT_MISMATCH: 2 },
  phaseMs: { collect: 900, compare: 20, reverify: 300 },
  error: null,
  ...over,
});

describe('VAN 등록 대조 (F9)', () => {
  it('실행 목록과 수동 실행(202). 진행 중인 실행이 있으면 실행 버튼이 꺼지고 다시 읽기를 알린다', async () => {
    let runs = [run()];
    const { calls } = mockApi({
      'GET /van-reconciliations': () => ({ body: { items: runs, nextBeforeId: null } }),
      'POST /van-reconciliations': () => {
        runs = [run({ id: 4, status: 'RUNNING', finishedAt: null }), ...runs];
        return { status: 202, body: runs[0] };
      },
    });
    loginAs();
    renderPage(<VanRunsPage />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: '#3' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/reports/van/3');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText(/수집 급감 확인함/));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '대조 실행' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: '#4' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')!.body).toEqual({ acknowledgeDrop: true });
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '대조 실행' })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('대조 진행 중');
  });

  it('실행 상세: 요약, 종류 이름(kindLabel), 재검증 기본은 어긋남만, 더 보기는 afterId', async () => {
    const { calls } = mockApi({
      'GET /van-reconciliations/3': (c) => ({
        body: {
          ...run(),
          items: c.query.get('afterId')
            ? [{ id: 12, serialNo: 'VN-2', kind: 'NOT_REGISTERED', kindLabel: '미등록', ourValue: 'IN_SERVICE / A', vanValue: 'VAN에 없음', verified: true, reason: null }]
            : [{ id: 11, serialNo: 'VN-1', kind: 'MERCHANT_MISMATCH', kindLabel: '가맹점 다름', ourValue: 'IN_SERVICE / A', vanValue: 'ACTIVE / B', verified: true, reason: null }],
          nextAfterId: c.query.get('afterId') ? null : 11,
        },
      }),
    });
    renderPage(<VanRunDetailPage />, { route: '/reports/van/3', path: '/reports/van/:id' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('가맹점 다름')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('후보 5 → 어긋남 3, 재검증으로 풀림 2, 확인 불가 0')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'MERCHANT_MISMATCH (2)' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls[0].query.get('verified')).toBe('true');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '더 보기' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('미등록')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls.at(-1)!.query.get('afterId')).toBe('11');
  });
});

describe('발주 입고 대조 (F10)', () => {
  it('발주를 고르면 대조(명세 차이·빠진 것·명세 밖)를 보여 주고, 확인하면 마감한다', async () => {
    const { calls } = mockApi({
      'GET /terminal-models': () => ({ body: [{ code: 'SN-100', name: '유선 카운터형', requiresReinspection: false }] }),
      'GET /purchase-orders': () => ({
        body: {
          items: [{ id: 5, poNo: 'SE0005', modelCode: 'SN-100', quantity: 10, status: 'RECEIVING', orderedBy: 'kim', createdAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z', closedAt: null, closedBy: null }],
          nextBeforeId: null,
        },
      }),
      'GET /purchase-orders/SE0005/reconciliation': () => ({
        body: { poNo: 'SE0005', modelCode: 'SN-100', status: 'RECEIVING', ordered: 10, noted: 9, received: 7, shortfall: 1, missing: ['SE5-8', 'SE5-9'], unexpected: ['ZZ-1'] },
      }),
      'POST /purchase-orders/SE0005/close': () => ({ body: {} }),
    });
    loginAs();
    renderPage(<PurchaseOrdersPage />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'SE0005' }));
    const panel = await screen.findByRole('generic', { name: '입고 대조' });
    expect(await within(panel).findByText('SE5-8, SE5-9')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(panel).getByText('ZZ-1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(panel).getByText('(발주와 차이 1)')).toBeInTheDocument();
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    await userEvent.click(within(panel).getByRole('button', { name: '마감' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.some((c) => c.path === '/purchase-orders/SE0005/close' && c.method === 'POST')).toBe(true));
  });
});

describe('주간 운영 리포트 (F11, decisions/0038)', () => {
  const report = (over: Partial<WeeklyReportView> = {}): WeeklyReportView => ({
    weekStart: '2026-10-19',
    status: 'SENT',
    fileName: '단말온-주간리포트-2026-10-19.xlsx',
    fileSize: 12345,
    generatedAt: '2026-10-26T00:00:00.000Z',
    sentAt: '2026-10-26T00:00:01.000Z',
    attempts: 1,
    lastError: null,
    alreadySent: false,
    queued: false,
    ...over,
  });

  it('지난주 월요일(KST)을 기본으로 둔다', () => {
    expect(lastWeekMonday('2026-10-27')).toBe('2026-10-19'); // 화요일
    expect(lastWeekMonday('2026-10-26')).toBe('2026-10-19'); // 월요일
    expect(lastWeekMonday('2026-11-01')).toBe('2026-10-19'); // 일요일
  });

  it('목록·내려받기 링크, 못 보낸 주는 다시 보내기(202 → 대기열 표시와 다시 읽기)', async () => {
    let rows = [report(), report({ weekStart: '2026-10-12', status: 'GENERATED', fileSize: 9999, sentAt: null, attempts: 2, lastError: 'PartnerTransientError: 톡톡메신저 응답 500' })];
    const { calls } = mockApi({
      'GET /reports/weekly': () => ({ body: { items: rows, nextBefore: null } }),
      'POST /reports/weekly': (c) => {
        rows = rows.map((r) => (r.weekStart === (c.body as { weekStart: string }).weekStart ? { ...r, queued: true } : r));
        return { status: 202, body: { ...rows[1], queued: true } };
      },
    });
    loginAs();
    renderPage(<WeeklyReportsPage />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'xlsx (12,345바이트)' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/api/reports/weekly/2026-10-19/file');
    expect(screen.getByText('PartnerTransientError: 톡톡메신저 응답 500')).toHaveClass('bad');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '다시 보내기' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('대기열')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')!.body).toEqual({ weekStart: '2026-10-12' });
    expect(screen.getByText('2026-10-12 주: 보내기 대기열에 넣었습니다.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/보내는 중 — 2초마다/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: '다시 보내기' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`apps/admin/src/components/RunningPoller.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

/** 진행 중인 비동기 작업(VAN 대조, 주간 리포트 보내기)이 있으면 2초마다 다시 읽는다(202 뒤 결과를 보려고, decisions/0038) */
export const RUNNING_POLL_MS = 2_000;

/** 그려져 있는 동안 ms마다 refetch를 부른다. 다 끝나면 화면이 이 컴포넌트를 빼서 멈춘다 */
export function RunningPoller({ refetch, ms = RUNNING_POLL_MS, text = '진행 중' }: { refetch: () => void; ms?: number; text?: string }) {
  const latest = useRef(refetch);
  latest.current = refetch;
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => latest.current(), ms);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [ms]);
  return (
    <span className="muted" role="status">
      {text} — {ms / 1000}초마다 다시 읽는 중
    </span>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/reports/AnomaliesPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useInfiniteQuery, useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '../../api/client';
import { ErrorBox } from '../../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../../components/MoreButton';
import { WriteButton } from '../../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst } from '../../lib/format';
import { label, TERMINAL_STATUS_LABEL } from '../../lib/labels';

interface AnomalyRuleView {
  rule: string;
  label: string;
  days: number | null;
}

interface AnomalyView {
  id: number;
  serialNo: string | null;
  status: string | null;
  rule: string;
  ruleLabel: string;
  detectedAt: string;
  lastSeenAt: string;
  resolvedAt: string | null;
  detail: { days?: number };
}

interface ScanSummary {
  detected: number;
  continuing: number;
  resolved: number;
  newSerials: string[];
}

/** 이상 단말기(F4): 규칙·열림으로 거르고 afterId로 이어 본다(서버 목록은 오름차순 키셋, decisions/0033). 바로 찾기는 동기(decisions/0038) */
export function AnomaliesPage() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [rule, setRule] = useState('');
  const [open, setOpen] = useState('true');
  const rules = useQuery({ queryKey: ['anomaly-rules'], queryFn: () => api<AnomalyRuleView[]>('/anomaly-rules'), staleTime: Infinity });
  const list = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: ['anomalies', rule, open],
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) =>
      api<{ items: AnomalyView[]; nextAfterId: number | null }>('/anomalies', { query: { rule, open, afterId: pageParam ?? undefined, limit: 100 } }),
    initialPageParam: null as number | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextAfterId,
  });
  const scan = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<ScanSummary>('/anomaly-scans', { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['anomalies'] }),
  });
  const items = list.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];

  return (
    <section>
      <h2>이상 단말기</h2>
      <div className="filters">
        <select aria-label="규칙" value={rule} onChange={(e) => setRule(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">규칙 전체</option>
          {rules.data?.map((r) => (
            <option key={r.rule} value={r.rule}>
              {r.label}
              {r.days === null ? '' : ` (${r.days}일)`}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select aria-label="열림" value={open} onChange={(e) => setOpen(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="true">열린 것</option>
          <option value="false">닫힌 것</option>
          <option value="">전체</option>
        </select>
        <WriteButton disabled={scan.isPending} onClick={() => scan.mutate()}>
          {scan.isPending ? '찾는 중' : '지금 찾기'}
        </WriteButton>
      </div>
      {scan.data ? (
        <p role="status">
          새로 {scan.data.detected}건, 계속 {scan.data.continuing}건, 풀림 {scan.data.resolved}건
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <ErrorBox error={list.error ?? scan.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>단말기</th>
            <th>지금 상태</th>
            <th>규칙</th>
            <th>처음 걸림</th>
            <th>마지막 확인</th>
            <th>풀림</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {items.map((a) => (
            <tr key={a.id}>
              <td>{a.serialNo ? <Link to={`/terminals/${encodeURIComponent(a.serialNo)}`}>{a.serialNo}</Link> : '(지워진 단말기)'}</td>
              <td>{label(TERMINAL_STATUS_LABEL, a.status)}</td>
              <td>
                {a.ruleLabel}
                {a.detail.days === undefined ? '' : ` · ${a.detail.days}일`}
              </td>
              <td>{formatKst(a.detectedAt)}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(a.lastSeenAt)}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(a.resolvedAt)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/reports/VanRunsPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '../../api/client';
import { itemsOf, useBeforeIdList } from '../../api/lists';
import { ErrorBox } from '../../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../../components/MoreButton';
import { RunningPoller } from '../../components/RunningPoller';
import { WriteButton } from '../../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst } from '../../lib/format';

export interface VanRunView {
  id: number;
  status: string;
  trigger: string;
  requestedBy: string;
  acknowledgeDrop: boolean;
  startedAt: string;
  heartbeatAt: string;
  finishedAt: string | null;
  pages: number;
  fetched: number;
  retries: number;
  candidates: number;
  confirmed: number;
  resolved: number;
  unverifiable: number;
  counts: Record<string, number>;
  phaseMs: { collect?: number; compare?: number; reverify?: number };
  error: string | null;
}

/** VAN 등록 대조(F9) 실행 목록과 수동 실행. 실행은 비동기(202) — 목록이 RUNNING이면 저절로 다시 읽는다 */
export function VanRunsPage() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [acknowledgeDrop, setAcknowledgeDrop] = useState(false);
  const list = useBeforeIdList<VanRunView>('van-runs', '/van-reconciliations');
  const runs = itemsOf(list.data);
  const running = runs.some((r) => r.status === 'RUNNING');
  const start = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<VanRunView>('/van-reconciliations', { method: 'POST', body: { acknowledgeDrop } }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      setAcknowledgeDrop(false);
      return client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['van-runs'] });
    },
  });

  return (
    <section>
      <h2>VAN 등록 대조</h2>
      <div className="filters">
        <label>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={acknowledgeDrop} onChange={(e) => setAcknowledgeDrop(e.target.checked)} /> 수집 급감 확인함(이번 실행만 급감 검사 건너뜀)
        </label>
        <WriteButton className="primary" disabled={start.isPending || running} onClick={() => start.mutate()}>
          대조 실행
        </WriteButton>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void list.refetch()}>
          새로고침
        </button>
        {running ? <RunningPoller refetch={() => void list.refetch()} text="대조 진행 중" /> : null}
      </div>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error ?? start.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>번호</th>
            <th>상태</th>
            <th>시작</th>
            <th>끝</th>
            <th>VAN 등록</th>
            <th>후보</th>
            <th>어긋남</th>
            <th>풀림</th>
            <th>확인 불가</th>
            <th>요청</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {runs.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.id}>
              <td>
                <Link to={`/reports/van/${r.id}`}>#{r.id}</Link>
              </td>
              <td className={r.status === 'FAILED' ? 'bad' : undefined} title={r.error ?? undefined}>
                {r.status}
              </td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.startedAt)}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.finishedAt)}</td>
              <td>{r.fetched}</td>
              <td>{r.candidates}</td>
              <td>{r.confirmed}</td>
              <td>{r.resolved}</td>
              <td>{r.unverifiable}</td>
              <td>
                {r.trigger === 'SCHEDULED' ? '주기' : r.requestedBy}
                {r.acknowledgeDrop ? ' (급감 확인)' : ''}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/reports/VanRunDetailPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useInfiniteQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '../../api/client';
import { ErrorBox } from '../../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../../components/MoreButton';
import { RunningPoller } from '../../components/RunningPoller';
import { formatKst } from '../../lib/format';
import type { VanRunView } from './VanRunsPage';

interface VanItemView {
  id: number;
  serialNo: string;
  kind: string;
  kindLabel: string;
  ourValue: string;
  vanValue: string;
  verified: boolean;
  reason: string | null;
}

type RunWithItems = VanRunView & { items: VanItemView[]; nextAfterId: number | null };

/** VAN 대조 실행 하나: 요약(종류별 수·단계별 시간)과 결과 항목(종류·재검증 여부로 거르고 afterId로 이어 본다) */
export function VanRunDetailPage() {
  const id = Number(useParams().id);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState('');
  const [verified, setVerified] = useState('true');
  const detail = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: ['van-run', id, kind, verified],
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) =>
      api<RunWithItems>(`/van-reconciliations/${id}`, { query: { kind, verified, afterId: pageParam ?? undefined, limit: 100 } }),
    initialPageParam: null as number | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextAfterId,
  });
  const run = detail.data?.pages[0];
  const items = detail.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];

  return (
    <section>
      <p>
        <Link to="/reports/van">← VAN 대조 목록</Link>
      </p>
      <h2>VAN 대조 #{id}</h2>
      <ErrorBox error={detail.error} />
      {run ? (
        <div className="panel">
          <dl className="info">
            <dt>상태</dt>
            <dd>
              <strong className={run.status === 'FAILED' ? 'bad' : undefined}>{run.status}</strong>
              {run.status === 'RUNNING' ? <RunningPoller refetch={() => void detail.refetch()} text="대조 진행 중" /> : null}
            </dd>
            <dt>기간</dt>
            <dd>
              {formatKst(run.startedAt)} ~ {formatKst(run.finishedAt)}
            </dd>
            <dt>수집</dt>
            <dd>
              {run.pages}쪽 {run.fetched}건(다시 부름 {run.retries}번)
            </dd>
            <dt>결과</dt>
            <dd>
              후보 {run.candidates} → 어긋남 {run.confirmed}, 재검증으로 풀림 {run.resolved}, 확인 불가 {run.unverifiable}
            </dd>
            <dt>단계 시간</dt>
            <dd>
              수집 {run.phaseMs.collect ?? '-'}ms · 비교 {run.phaseMs.compare ?? '-'}ms · 재검증 {run.phaseMs.reverify ?? '-'}ms
            </dd>
            {run.error ? (
              <>
                <dt>실패 이유</dt>
                <dd className="bad">{run.error}</dd>
              </>
            ) : null}
          </dl>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <div className="filters">
        <select aria-label="종류" value={kind} onChange={(e) => setKind(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">종류 전체</option>
          {Object.entries(run?.counts ?? {}).map(([code, n]) => (
            <option key={code} value={code}>
              {code} ({n})
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select aria-label="재검증" value={verified} onChange={(e) => setVerified(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="true">어긋남(재검증 뒤에도)</option>
          <option value="false">풀림·확인 불가</option>
          <option value="">전체</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>시리얼</th>
            <th>종류</th>
            <th>우리</th>
            <th>VAN</th>
            <th>재검증</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {items.map((it) => (
            <tr key={it.id}>
              <td>{it.serialNo}</td>
              <td>{it.kindLabel}</td>
              <td>{it.ourValue}</td>
              <td>{it.vanValue}</td>
              <td>{it.verified ? '어긋남' : (it.reason ?? '풀림')}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={detail.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={detail.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={detail.fetchNextPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/reports/PurchaseOrdersPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../../api/client';
import { itemsOf, useBeforeIdList } from '../../api/lists';
import type { TerminalModelView } from '../../api/types';
import { ErrorBox } from '../../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../../components/MoreButton';
import { WriteButton } from '../../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst } from '../../lib/format';

interface PurchaseOrderView {
  id: number;
  poNo: string;
  modelCode: string;
  quantity: number;
  status: string;
  orderedBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
  closedAt: string | null;
  closedBy: string | null;
}

interface Reconciliation {
  poNo: string;
  modelCode: string;
  status: string;
  ordered: number;
  noted: number;
  received: number;
  shortfall: number;
  missing: string[];
  unexpected: string[];
}

/** 발주 대비 입고 대조(F10): 발주 목록, 발주하기, 고른 발주의 대조(명세·입고·빠진 것·명세 밖), 마감 */
export function PurchaseOrdersPage() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [modelCode, setModelCode] = useState('');
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState('10');
  const list = useBeforeIdList<PurchaseOrderView>('purchase-orders', '/purchase-orders');
  const models = useQuery({ queryKey: ['terminal-models'], queryFn: () => api<TerminalModelView[]>('/terminal-models'), staleTime: Infinity });
  const recon = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['purchase-order', selected, 'reconciliation'],
    queryFn: () => api<Reconciliation>(`/purchase-orders/${encodeURIComponent(selected!)}/reconciliation`),
    enabled: selected !== null,
  });
  const order = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<PurchaseOrderView>('/purchase-orders', { method: 'POST', body: { modelCode, quantity: Number(quantity) } }),
    onSuccess: async (po) => {
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['purchase-orders'] });
      setSelected(po.poNo);
    },
  });
  const close = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (poNo: string) => api(`/purchase-orders/${encodeURIComponent(poNo)}/close`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['purchase-orders'] });
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['purchase-order'] });
    },
  });
  const qty = Number(quantity);

  return (
    <section>
      <h2>발주 입고 대조</h2>
      <div className="row">
        <select aria-label="발주 모델" value={modelCode} onChange={(e) => setModelCode(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">모델 선택</option>
          {models.data?.map((m) => (
            <option key={m.code} value={m.code}>
              {m.code} {m.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <input aria-label="수량" type="number" min={1} max={5000} value={quantity} onChange={(e) => setQuantity(e.target.value)} />
        <WriteButton disabled={!modelCode || !Number.isInteger(qty) || qty < 1 || qty > 5000 || order.isPending} onClick={() => order.mutate()}>
          발주하기
        </WriteButton>
      </div>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error ?? order.error ?? close.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>발주번호</th>
            <th>모델</th>
            <th>수량</th>
            <th>상태</th>
            <th>발주</th>
            <th>마감</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {itemsOf(list.data).map((p) => (
            <tr key={p.id} aria-selected={selected === p.poNo}>
              <td>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setSelected(p.poNo)}>
                  {p.poNo}
                </button>
              </td>
              <td>{p.modelCode}</td>
              <td>{p.quantity}</td>
              <td>{p.status}</td>
              <td>
                {formatKst(p.createdAt)} · {p.orderedBy}
              </td>
              <td>{p.closedAt ? `${formatKst(p.closedAt)} · ${p.closedBy}` : '-'}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
      {selected ? (
        <div className="panel" aria-label="입고 대조">
          <h3>발주 {selected} 대조</h3>
          <ErrorBox error={recon.error} />
          {recon.data ? (
            <>
              <dl className="info">
                <dt>발주 수량</dt>
                <dd>{recon.data.ordered}</dd>
                <dt>납품 명세</dt>
                <dd>
                  {recon.data.noted}
                  {recon.data.shortfall !== 0 ? <span className="bad"> (발주와 차이 {recon.data.shortfall})</span> : null}
                </dd>
                <dt>입고</dt>
                <dd>{recon.data.received}</dd>
                <dt>명세에 있는데 안 들어옴</dt>
                <dd>{recon.data.missing.length ? recon.data.missing.join(', ') : '없음'}</dd>
                <dt>명세 밖이라 거절</dt>
                <dd>{recon.data.unexpected.length ? recon.data.unexpected.join(', ') : '없음'}</dd>
              </dl>
              {recon.data.status !== 'CLOSED' ? (
                <WriteButton
                  className="danger"
                  disabled={close.isPending}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (window.confirm(`발주 ${selected}을(를) 마감할까요? 마감하면 입고 스캔을 받지 않습니다.`)) close.mutate(selected);
                  }}
                >
                  마감
                </WriteButton>
              ) : (
                <p className="muted">마감된 발주입니다.</p>
              )}
            </>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/reports/WeeklyReportsPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useInfiniteQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, downloadUrl } from '../../api/client';
import { ErrorBox } from '../../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../../components/MoreButton';
import { RunningPoller } from '../../components/RunningPoller';
import { WriteButton } from '../../components/WriteButton';
import { formatKst, kstToday } from '../../lib/format';

export interface WeeklyReportView {
  weekStart: string;
  status: string;
  fileName: string | null;
  fileSize: number | null;
  generatedAt: string | null;
  sentAt: string | null;
  attempts: number;
  lastError: string | null;
  alreadySent: boolean;
  queued: boolean;
}

/** 오늘(KST)이 속한 주의 앞 주 월요일 'YYYY-MM-DD' — 주간 리포트의 기본 대상(서버의 previousKstWeek와 같은 뜻) */
export function lastWeekMonday(today = kstToday()): string {
  const d = new Date(`${today}T00:00:00Z`);
  const sinceMonday = (d.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7;
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - sinceMonday - 7);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/**
 * 주간 운영 리포트(F11): 주 목록, 보내기(비동기 202 — 대기열에 있는 동안 2초마다 다시 읽는다, decisions/0038), xlsx 내려받기.
 * 끝나지 않은 주는 서버가 422 WEEK_NOT_ENDED로 거절한다.
 */
export function WeeklyReportsPage() {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [weekStart, setWeekStart] = useState(lastWeekMonday());
  const list = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: ['weekly-reports'],
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => api<{ items: WeeklyReportView[]; nextBefore: string | null }>('/reports/weekly', { query: { before: pageParam ?? undefined } }),
    initialPageParam: null as string | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextBefore,
  });
  const send = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (week: string) => api<WeeklyReportView>('/reports/weekly', { method: 'POST', body: { weekStart: week } }),
    onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['weekly-reports'] }),
  });
  const items = list.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];
  const queued = items.some((r) => r.queued);

  return (
    <section>
      <h2>주간 운영 리포트</h2>
      <div className="filters">
        <label>
          주(월요일){' '}
          <input aria-label="보낼 주" type="date" value={weekStart} onChange={(e) => setWeekStart(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <WriteButton className="primary" disabled={!weekStart || send.isPending} onClick={() => send.mutate(weekStart)}>
          보내기
        </WriteButton>
        {queued ? <RunningPoller refetch={() => void list.refetch()} text="보내는 중" /> : null}
      </div>
      {send.data ? (
        <p role="status">
          {send.data.weekStart} 주: {send.data.alreadySent ? '이미 보낸 주입니다.' : '보내기 대기열에 넣었습니다.'}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <ErrorBox error={list.error ?? send.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>주</th>
            <th>상태</th>
            <th>보낸 시각</th>
            <th>시도</th>
            <th>마지막 오류</th>
            <th>파일</th>
            <th />
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {items.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.weekStart}>
              <td>{r.weekStart}</td>
              <td>
                {r.status}
                {r.queued ? <span className="badge">대기열</span> : null}
              </td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.sentAt)}</td>
              <td>{r.attempts}</td>
              <td className={r.lastError ? 'bad' : undefined}>{r.lastError ?? ''}</td>
              <td>
                {r.fileSize ? (
                  <a href={downloadUrl(`/reports/weekly/${r.weekStart}/file`)} download>
                    xlsx ({r.fileSize.toLocaleString()}바이트)
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  '-'
                )}
              </td>
              <td>
                {r.status !== 'SENT' && !r.queued ? (
                  <WriteButton disabled={send.isPending} onClick={() => send.mutate(r.weekStart)}>
                    다시 보내기
                  </WriteButton>
                ) : null}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router';
import { Layout } from './components/Layout';
import { AnomaliesPage } from './pages/reports/AnomaliesPage';
import { PurchaseOrdersPage } from './pages/reports/PurchaseOrdersPage';
import { VanRunDetailPage } from './pages/reports/VanRunDetailPage';
import { VanRunsPage } from './pages/reports/VanRunsPage';
import { WeeklyReportsPage } from './pages/reports/WeeklyReportsPage';
import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { RolloutsPage } from './pages/RolloutsPage';
import { ScanPage } from './pages/ScanPage';
import { ServiceTicketDetailPage } from './pages/ServiceTicketDetailPage';
import { ServiceTicketsPage } from './pages/ServiceTicketsPage';
import { TerminalDetailPage } from './pages/TerminalDetailPage';
import { TerminalListPage } from './pages/TerminalListPage';

/** 화면 경로. 메뉴(nav.ts)와 함께 고친다 */
export function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route index element={<HomePage />} />
        <Route path="terminals" element={<TerminalListPage />} />
        <Route path="terminals/:serialNo" element={<TerminalDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="scans" element={<ScanPage />} />
        <Route path="service-tickets" element={<ServiceTicketsPage />} />
        <Route path="service-tickets/:id" element={<ServiceTicketDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="firmware-rollouts" element={<RolloutsPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/anomalies" element={<AnomaliesPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/van" element={<VanRunsPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/van/:id" element={<VanRunDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/purchase-orders" element={<PurchaseOrdersPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/weekly" element={<WeeklyReportsPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<p>없는 화면입니다.</p>} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  );
}
```

`docs/decisions/0041-…`에 보탠다:
- 202 작업(VAN 대조, 주간 리포트)은 화면이 2초마다 다시 읽는다(`RunningPoller`) — 진행 중인 것이 목록에 보일 때만 돌고, 끝나면(RUNNING·queued가 사라지면) 컴포넌트가 빠져 멈춘다. 웹소켓·SSE는 쓰지 않는다(decisions/0038과 같은 이유).
- 내려받기는 `<a href="/api/…/file" download>` — 브라우저가 프록시로 바로 받는다(GET이라 작업자 헤더가 필요 없다).

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi, renderPage } from '../test/render';
import { BatchHistoryPage } from './BatchHistoryPage';

describe('배치 실행 이력 (decisions/0039)', () => {
  it('택배 수집 실행: 큐·결과로 거르고 오류는 빨갛게, 탭을 바꾸면 VAN 대조·주간 리포트 이력', async () => {
    const { calls } = mockApi({
      'GET /sync-runs': () => ({
        body: {
          items: [
            { id: 9, job: 'track-bungae', shipmentId: 1, trackingNo: 'BG-1', attempt: 2, worker: 'pid-1', outcome: 'ERROR', startedAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z', finishedAt: null, fetched: 0, inserted: 0, duplicated: 0, detail: '응답 500' },
          ],
          nextBeforeId: null,
        },
      }),
      'GET /van-reconciliations': () => ({
        body: { items: [{ id: 2, status: 'FAILED', trigger: 'SCHEDULED', requestedBy: 'system:van-reconcile', startedAt: '2026-10-27T00:00:00.000Z', finishedAt: null, confirmed: 0 }], nextBeforeId: null },
      }),
      'GET /reports/weekly': () => ({
        body: { items: [{ weekStart: '2026-10-19', status: 'SENT', attempts: 1, generatedAt: null, sentAt: '2026-10-26T00:00:00.000Z', lastError: null }], nextBefore: null },
      }),
    });
    renderPage(<BatchHistoryPage />, { route: '/batch-runs', path: '/batch-runs' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('응답 500')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('cell', { name: 'ERROR' })).toHaveClass('bad');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('결과'), 'ERROR');
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/sync-runs').at(-1)!.query.get('outcome')).toBe('ERROR'));

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'VAN 대조' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: '#2' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/reports/van/2');
    expect(screen.getByText('주기')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '주간 리포트' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: '2026-10-19' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '주간 리포트' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
  });
});
```

`apps/admin/src/pages/BatchHistoryPage.tsx`(새, 전체):
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useInfiniteQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { itemsOf, useBeforeIdList } from '../api/lists';
import { ErrorBox } from '../components/ErrorBox';
import { MoreButton } from '../components/MoreButton';
import { formatKst } from '../lib/format';
import type { VanRunView } from './reports/VanRunsPage';
import type { WeeklyReportView } from './reports/WeeklyReportsPage';

interface SyncRunView {
  id: number;
  job: string;
  shipmentId: number | null;
  trackingNo: string | null;
  attempt: number;
  worker: string;
  outcome: string;
  startedAt: string;
  finishedAt: string | null;
  fetched: number;
  inserted: number;
  duplicated: number;
  detail: string | null;
}

const TABS = [
  { key: 'sync', label: '택배 수집' },
  { key: 'van', label: 'VAN 대조' },
  { key: 'weekly', label: '주간 리포트' },
] as const;
type Tab = (typeof TABS)[number]['key'];

const OUTCOMES = ['OK', 'DELIVERED', 'NOOP', 'SKIPPED', 'ERROR'];

/**
 * 배치 실행 이력: 실행마다 행을 남기는 배치 세 가지(택배 수집 SyncRun, VAN 대조 실행, 주간 리포트 주별 행).
 * 이상 단말기 찾기는 실행 기록 표가 없다 — 결과는 이상 단말기 화면(열린 이상)과 알림으로 본다(decisions/0039).
 */
export function BatchHistoryPage() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = (TABS.some((t) => t.key === params.get('tab')) ? params.get('tab') : 'sync') as Tab;
  return (
    <section>
      <h2>배치 실행 이력</h2>
      <div className="tabs" role="tablist">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button key={t.key} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t.key} onClick={() => setParams({ tab: t.key })}>
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {tab === 'sync' ? <SyncRuns /> : tab === 'van' ? <VanRuns /> : <WeeklyRuns />}
      <p className="muted">이상 단말기 찾기는 실행 기록을 따로 남기지 않습니다. 결과는 이상 단말기 화면에서 봅니다.</p>
    </section>
  );
}

function SyncRuns() {
  const [job, setJob] = useState('');
  const [outcome, setOutcome] = useState('');
  const list = useBeforeIdList<SyncRunView>('sync-runs', '/sync-runs', { job, outcome });
  return (
    <>
      <div className="filters">
        <select aria-label="큐" value={job} onChange={(e) => setJob(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">큐 전체</option>
          <option value="track-bungae">track-bungae(번개택배)</option>
          <option value="track-neurimbo">track-neurimbo(느림보물류)</option>
        </select>
        <select aria-label="결과" value={outcome} onChange={(e) => setOutcome(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">결과 전체</option>
          {OUTCOMES.map((o) => (
            <option key={o} value={o}>
              {o}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>번호</th>
            <th>큐</th>
            <th>운송장</th>
            <th>시도</th>
            <th>결과</th>
            <th>받음/새로/중복</th>
            <th>시작</th>
            <th>워커</th>
            <th>내용</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {itemsOf(list.data).map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.id}>
              <td>{r.id}</td>
              <td>{r.job}</td>
              <td>{r.trackingNo ?? '-'}</td>
              <td>{r.attempt}</td>
              <td className={r.outcome === 'ERROR' ? 'bad' : undefined}>{r.outcome}</td>
              <td>
                {r.fetched}/{r.inserted}/{r.duplicated}
              </td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.startedAt)}</td>
              <td>{r.worker}</td>
              <td>{r.detail ?? ''}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </>
  );
}

function VanRuns() {
  const list = useBeforeIdList<VanRunView>('van-runs', '/van-reconciliations');
  return (
    <>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>번호</th>
            <th>상태</th>
            <th>시작</th>
            <th>끝</th>
            <th>요청</th>
            <th>어긋남</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {itemsOf(list.data).map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.id}>
              <td>
                <Link to={`/reports/van/${r.id}`}>#{r.id}</Link>
              </td>
              <td className={r.status === 'FAILED' ? 'bad' : undefined}>{r.status}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.startedAt)}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.finishedAt)}</td>
              <td>{r.trigger === 'SCHEDULED' ? '주기' : r.requestedBy}</td>
              <td>{r.confirmed}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </>
  );
}

function WeeklyRuns() {
  const list = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: ['weekly-reports'],
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => api<{ items: WeeklyReportView[]; nextBefore: string | null }>('/reports/weekly', { query: { before: pageParam ?? undefined } }),
    initialPageParam: null as string | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextBefore,
  });
  return (
    <>
      <ErrorBox error={list.error} />
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>주</th>
            <th>상태</th>
            <th>시도</th>
            <th>만든 시각</th>
            <th>보낸 시각</th>
            <th>마지막 오류</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {(list.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? []).map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.weekStart}>
              <td>
                <Link to="/reports/weekly">{r.weekStart}</Link>
              </td>
              <td>{r.status}</td>
              <td>{r.attempts}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.generatedAt)}</td>
              <td>{formatKst(r.sentAt)}</td>
              <td className={r.lastError ? 'bad' : undefined}>{r.lastError ?? ''}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <MoreButton hasNextPage={list.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={list.isFetchingNextPage} fetchNextPage={list.fetchNextPage} />
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router';
import { Layout } from './components/Layout';
import { BatchHistoryPage } from './pages/BatchHistoryPage';
import { AnomaliesPage } from './pages/reports/AnomaliesPage';
import { PurchaseOrdersPage } from './pages/reports/PurchaseOrdersPage';
import { VanRunDetailPage } from './pages/reports/VanRunDetailPage';
import { VanRunsPage } from './pages/reports/VanRunsPage';
import { WeeklyReportsPage } from './pages/reports/WeeklyReportsPage';
import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { RolloutsPage } from './pages/RolloutsPage';
import { ScanPage } from './pages/ScanPage';
import { ServiceTicketDetailPage } from './pages/ServiceTicketDetailPage';
import { ServiceTicketsPage } from './pages/ServiceTicketsPage';
import { TerminalDetailPage } from './pages/TerminalDetailPage';
import { TerminalListPage } from './pages/TerminalListPage';

/** 화면 경로. 메뉴(nav.ts)와 함께 고친다 */
export function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route index element={<HomePage />} />
        <Route path="terminals" element={<TerminalListPage />} />
        <Route path="terminals/:serialNo" element={<TerminalDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="scans" element={<ScanPage />} />
        <Route path="service-tickets" element={<ServiceTicketsPage />} />
        <Route path="service-tickets/:id" element={<ServiceTicketDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="firmware-rollouts" element={<RolloutsPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/anomalies" element={<AnomaliesPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/van" element={<VanRunsPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/van/:id" element={<VanRunDetailPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/purchase-orders" element={<PurchaseOrdersPage />} />
        <Route path="reports/weekly" element={<WeeklyReportsPage />} />
        <Route path="batch-runs" element={<BatchHistoryPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<p>없는 화면입니다.</p>} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  );
}
```

- api는 `apps/api/dist`를 실행하므로 미리보기 전에 `apps/api`에서 `npm run build`, partners도 `npm run build`(Step 1이 이미 했다).
- `--env-file`을 둘 주면 뒤 파일이 이긴다(사전 검증) — 저장소 `.env`의 `RUN_SCHEDULERS=1`을 `infra/preview.env`가 0으로 덮는다. 워커는 켜져 있어 202 작업(주간 리포트 보내기, VAN 대조)이 돈다.

- 미리보기로 띄운 서버는 확인이 끝나면 미리보기 도구로 끈다(`preview_stop`). 개발 DB를 쓴다(실서버 확인과 같다).

- [ ] **Step 3: 실서버 확인(개발 DB, api·워커 + partners + 어드민 개발 서버 프록시, 주기 작업 끔)**

개발 DB는 Day 5 마이그레이션까지 적용돼 있어야 한다(`npm run db:migration:check` → 최신 상태). 아래 스크립트를 스크래치패드(커밋하지 않음)에 `smoke-day6.sh`로 두고 `apps/api`에서 Git Bash로 `bash <경로>/smoke-day6.sh`. 모든 요청은 어드민 개발 서버의 `/api` 프록시를 거친다(브라우저와 같은 길). 포트는 `AP`·`PP`·`VP`, 주간 리포트 주는 `WEEK`(기본 2026-10-12)로 바꾼다.
```bash
# Day 6 실서버 확인: api(워커 포함, 주기 작업 끔) + partners + 어드민 개발 서버(프록시). apps/api에서 Git Bash로 실행: bash <경로>/smoke-day6.sh
# 모든 요청은 어드민 개발 서버의 /api 프록시를 거친다(브라우저와 같은 길, decisions/0037).
AP=${AP:-3000}; PP=${PP:-4010}; VP=${VP:-5173}; OUT=${OUT:-.}; WEEK=${WEEK:-2026-10-12}
V=localhost:$VP; A=$V/api; H='Content-Type: application/json'; S=$(date +%H%M%S)
# 작업자 이름 '김운영'(URL 인코딩)
O='X-Operator: %EA%B9%80%EC%9A%B4%EC%98%81'
show() { node -e "let s='';process.stdin.on('data',d=>s+=d).on('end',()=>{const t=JSON.parse(s);console.log($1)})"; }

PARTNERS_PORT=$PP node ../partners/dist/main.js > $OUT/smoke6-partners.log 2>&1 & P1=$!
API_PORT=$AP PARTNERS_URL=http://127.0.0.1:$PP RUN_SCHEDULERS=0 RELAY_INTERVAL_MS=1000 node dist/main.js > $OUT/smoke6-api.log 2>&1 & P2=$!
ADMIN_API_URL=http://localhost:$AP node ../admin/node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js ../admin --port $VP --strictPort > $OUT/smoke6-admin.log 2>&1 & P3=$!
echo "PIDS partners=$P1 api=$P2 admin=$P3"
trap 'kill $P3 $P2 $P1 2>/dev/null' EXIT
# -f: api가 뜨기 전에는 프록시가 502를 준다(admin 로그에 ECONNREFUSED가 남는다). 200이 될 때까지 기다린다
n=0; until curl -sf -o /dev/null http://$A/health; do sleep 0.5; n=$((n+1)); [ $n -gt 60 ] && { echo "프록시 너머 api 안 뜸"; exit 1; }; done
echo "S=$S WEEK=$WEEK"

echo '--- 1. 어드민 첫 화면과 프록시(/api → api, 앞의 /api를 뗀다)'
curl -s http://$V/ | grep -o '<title>[^<]*</title>'
curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' http://$A/health

echo '--- 2. 등록(한글 작업자) → 상세의 할 수 있는 일(RECEIVED: 검수만) → 검수 → 재고 → 상세(배정 가능)'
N=D6-$S-1
curl -s -X POST http://$A/terminals -H "$H" -H "$O" -d "{\"serialNo\":\"$N\",\"modelCode\":\"SN-100\"}" -o /dev/null -w 'register %{http_code}\n'
curl -s http://$A/terminals/$N | show 't.status, t.version, JSON.stringify(t.allowedTransitions), JSON.stringify(t.actions)'
curl -s -X POST http://$A/terminals/$N/inspections -H "$H" -H "$O" -d '{"results":[{"itemCode":"POWER","passed":true},{"itemCode":"NETWORK","passed":true},{"itemCode":"CARD_READER","passed":true},{"itemCode":"PRINTER","passed":true}]}' -o /dev/null -w 'inspect %{http_code}\n'
curl -s -X POST http://$A/terminals/$N/transitions -H "$H" -H "$O" -d '{"to":"IN_STOCK","reason":"smoke","expectedVersion":2}' -o /dev/null -w 'to IN_STOCK %{http_code}\n'
curl -s http://$A/terminals/$N | show 't.status, t.version, JSON.stringify(t.allowedTransitions), JSON.stringify(t.actions)'
curl -s http://$A/terminals/$N/history | show "t.map((h) => h.toStatus + '(' + h.actor + ')').join(' > ')"
curl -s http://$A/terminals/$N/inspections | show "'inspections', t.length, t[0].results.map((x) => x.label).join(',')"

echo '--- 3. 낡은 version으로 배정 → 409 VERSION_CONFLICT, 작업자 없이 쓰기 → 400'
curl -s -X POST http://$A/terminals/$N/allocations -H "$H" -H "$O" -d '{"merchantCode":"YEOKSAM-CAFE","expectedVersion":2}' -w ' %{http_code}\n'
curl -s -X POST http://$A/terminals/$N/allocations -H "$H" -d '{"merchantCode":"YEOKSAM-CAFE","expectedVersion":3}' -o /dev/null -w 'no operator %{http_code}\n'

echo '--- 4. 박스 스캔(회수 입고) 같은 scanId 두 번 → 201 다음 200 replayed'
SID=BOX-SMOKE6-$S
for i in 1 2; do
  curl -s -X POST http://$A/scans -H "$H" -H "$O" -d "{\"scanId\":\"$SID\",\"kind\":\"RETURN_IN\",\"serials\":[\"$N\",\"NOPE-$S\"]}" -o $OUT/smoke6-scan.json -w '%{http_code} '
  show 't.replayed, JSON.stringify(t.summary)' < $OUT/smoke6-scan.json
done

echo '--- 5. 화면용 목록(limit 2)'
for p in 'terminals?size=2' terminal-models anomaly-rules 'purchase-orders?limit=2' 'service-tickets?limit=2' 'firmware-rollouts?limit=2' 'van-reconciliations?limit=2' 'sync-runs?limit=2' 'reports/weekly?limit=2' 'anomalies?open=true&limit=2'; do
  curl -s "http://$A/$p" -o $OUT/smoke6-list.json -w "$p %{http_code} %{size_download}B\n"
done

echo "--- 6. 주간 리포트 보내기($WEEK 주) → 202 queued → job이 끝나면 SENT(이미 보낸 주면 200 alreadySent)"
curl -s -X POST http://$A/reports/weekly -H "$H" -H "$O" -d "{\"weekStart\":\"$WEEK\"}" -w ' %{http_code}\n'
n=0; until curl -s http://$A/reports/weekly/$WEEK | grep -q '"queued":false'; do sleep 0.5; n=$((n+1)); [ $n -gt 60 ] && { echo "보내기 job 안 끝남"; break; }; done
curl -s http://$A/reports/weekly/$WEEK; echo
curl -s -o $OUT/smoke6-report.xlsx -w "download: %{http_code} %{size_download} bytes %{content_type}\n" http://$A/reports/weekly/$WEEK/file

kill $P3 $P2 $P1
echo "--- 종료: kill $P3 $P2 $P1"
```

Expected(사전 검증에서 테스트 DB·포트 3100/4110/5273, `WEEK=2026-07-27`로 같은 흐름을 확인했다):
- 1: `<title>단말온 운영 어드민</title>`, `{"status":"ok"} 200`
- 2: `register 201`, `RECEIVED 1 [] {"allocate":false,"cancelAllocation":false,"ship":false,"inspect":true}`, `inspect 201`, `to IN_STOCK 200`, `IN_STOCK 3 [] {"allocate":true,"cancelAllocation":false,"ship":false,"inspect":false}`, `RECEIVED(김운영) > INSPECTED(김운영) > IN_STOCK(김운영)`(한글 작업자가 프록시를 지나 이력에 남음), `inspections 1 전원,유선 통신,카드리더,영수증 프린터`
- 3: `{"statusCode":409,"code":"VERSION_CONFLICT",…} 409`, `no operator 400`
- 4: `201 false {"INVALID_STATE":1,"NOT_FOUND":1}` 다음 `200 true {"INVALID_STATE":1,"NOT_FOUND":1}`(같은 scanId는 저장된 결과)
- 5: 열 개 목록 모두 `200`
- 6: 개발 DB에 그 주가 없거나 못 보냈으면 `{… "status":"PENDING", … "queued":true} 202` → 잠시 뒤 `{… "status":"SENT", "fileName":"단말온-주간리포트-<주>.xlsx", "fileSize":<수 KB>, "attempts":1, "lastError":null, … "queued":false}`, `download: 200 <fileSize> bytes application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`. 이미 보낸 주면 `{… "alreadySent":true, "queued":false} 200`(다른 `WEEK`로 한 번 더 돌려 202 경로를 본다 — 개발 DB에 2026-10-19는 Day 5에 SENT)
- `smoke6-api.log`에 `terminal-ops api listening on http://localhost:3000 (workers=true, schedulers=false, queuePrefix=terminal-ops)`, `weekly-report-send 끝: <주> 주 SENT`. `grep -icE "error|exception|warn" smoke6-api.log smoke6-partners.log`가 0. `smoke6-admin.log`에는 api가 뜨기 전 `http proxy error … ECONNREFUSED`가 몇 줄 있을 수 있다(대기 중 502 — 그 밖의 오류는 없어야 한다)

출력을 devlog에 붙인다. 끝나면 `netstat -ano | grep -E ':(3000|4010|5173) .*LISTENING'` 결과 없음을 확인하고 파일을 지운다(`rm smoke6-*.log smoke6-*.json smoke6-report.xlsx`).

- [ ] **Step 4: 화면 확인(내장 브라우저 — 컨트롤러가 한다)**

이 단계는 내장 브라우저 미리보기 도구가 있는 컨트롤러가 한다(서브에이전트는 건너뛰고 devlog에 "컨트롤러 확인 대기"라고 적는다). `terminal-ops-partners` → `terminal-ops-api` → `terminal-ops-admin` 순서로 띄우고 `http://localhost:5173`에서:
- 위쪽에 작업자 이름 저장 → 단말기 목록(필터·쪽) → Step 3이 만든 `D6-…` 상세(타임라인 KST, 할 수 있는 일 = 가맹점 배정) → 배정 → 상세가 ALLOCATED와 배정 취소·출고 버튼으로 바뀜
- 박스 스캔(회수 입고) 같은 박스 두 번 → 두 번째 "이미 처리된 박스"
- 리포트: 이상 단말기(지금 찾기), VAN 대조(실행 → 진행 중 다시 읽기 → COMPLETED), 주간 리포트(보내기 → 대기열 → SENT, xlsx 내려받기), 배치 실행 이력 탭
- 확인한 것과 이상하게 보인 것을 devlog에 적고, 띄운 세 서버를 끈다(포트 3000·4010·5173 리스너 없음)

- [ ] **Step 5: 하루 요약과 공부거리 정리**

devlog 맨 아래에 `## 6일차 요약`을 쓴다(5일차 요약과 같은 항목).
- 만든 것: 이월(레이블 상수 분리, 주간 리포트 job + 202, 날짜에 기대는 테스트), 화면용 API(상세의 allowedTransitions·actions, 가맹점 필터, 검수 기록, 모델·규칙 목록, beforeId 목록 5개, 주간 리포트 목록), `apps/admin`(화면 목록·경로), 미리보기 구성
- 테스트 수: api, admin, partners, device-sim(실제 값)
- 커밋 목록(`git log --oneline master..`)
- 실험 0013 결론 한 줄(잰 숫자로)
- 화면 구성 결정 요약: 프록시(CORS 없음), X-Operator 주입, 서버가 계산하는 가능한 전이, 키셋 "더 보기"·offset 쪽, 202 + 다시 읽기, 버전 표(decisions/0037~0041)
- 계획과 달라진 점, 남은 문제(스펙 8장 + 새로 생긴 것: 화면 운영 배포·인증 없음, 작업자 이름은 브라우저마다, 상태·큐 조건 목록은 인덱스 없이 PK 역순으로 거름, 이상 찾기 실행 기록 없음, Day 5에서 넘어온 것), 내일(Day 7 나머지 실험·README 결과 표·기록 정리) 시작 전에 필요한 것(설계안 8장 Day 7 행, 대시보드 캐시 실험 후보)

`docs/study/backlog.md`의 중복 항목을 합친다. 설계안 `../01_B_단말기생애주기_설계안.md` 8장 Day 6 행 끝에 `✅ 완료(커밋 범위)`를 표시한다(저장소 밖이라 커밋하지 않는다).

- [ ] **Step 6: 커밋**

```bash
git add infra/preview.env docs
git commit -m "docs: 6일차 마감 기록과 미리보기 실행 설정" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```
devlog 제목 시각을 커밋 시각과 맞춘다. 병합은 하지 않는다(브랜치 `day6-admin`에 남긴다 — 최종 리뷰 뒤).
